2026-10-09T10:06:43.5790614Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-09T10:06:43.5792680Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] Raise P3/D1 large-pool client concurrency from 72 to 88

Companion condition to ed8c534 (P3/D1, decode pool 393216, c72). Only the
client concurrency changes; topology, pools, image and all serving flags
are identical.

Why: c72 is planned near the prefill saturation point assuming ~14 ms
decode ITL. If the single decode worker's ITL is higher at a ~60-request
batch, residence time grows and c72 under-feeds prefill. c88 brackets that
case: at ~18 ms ITL the minimum residence is ~4.2 s and c88 still offers
about 21 req/s, while at ~14 ms ITL it adds roughly 0.6 s of prefill
queueing (TTFT well below 3 s). Running both points concurrently
measures the decode batch/ITL curve and the TTFT cost of extra queueing in
one wave.

The decode pool (about 87 admitted requests) covers c88 only together with
prefill-side residency; observe prealloc queue and token usage to see
whether admission, not prefill, becomes the cap.

Validation: full CPU pytest suite passes with the c88 expectation.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
